CREATE INDEX SGOAM.FK_ACTION_T_ACT_PREVU ON SGOAM.T_ACTIONS_PREVUES
(CODE_FAMILLE, NUM_ACTION, CODE_CLASSE, CODE_PARTIE_OUV)
TABLESPACE SGOAM
PCTFREE    10
INITRANS   2
MAXTRANS   255
STORAGE    (
            INITIAL          128K
            NEXT             1M
            MINEXTENTS       1
            MAXEXTENTS       UNLIMITED
            PCTINCREASE      0
            BUFFER_POOL      DEFAULT
           );
